### [v1.2.1](https://github.com/MakeShiftArtist/ifunny-api-types/compare/v1.2.0...v1.2.1) -  1 November 2026 

#### Chores And Housekeeping

- Chore(deps): Bump brace-expansion, eslint and typescript-eslint @dependabot[bot] 
- chore(devenv): update devenv @MakeShiftArtist 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump @humanfs/node from 0.16.6 to 0.16.8 @dependabot[bot] 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump handlebars from 4.7.9 to 4.7.10 @dependabot[bot] 
- chore(npm): bump package version @MakeShiftArtist 
- chore(prettier): add *.md to .prettierignore @MakeShiftArtist 
- chore(prettier): add .prettierignore @MakeShiftArtist 
- chore(devenv): add eslint git hook @MakeShiftArtist 

### [v1.2.0](https://github.com/MakeShiftArtist/ifunny-api-types/compare/v1.1.2...v1.2.0) -  1 November 2026 

#### New Features

- feat(types): add invite/kick RPC payload types and composition functions @Eva Harris 
- feat(types): add chat API types (APIChatChannel, APIChatMessage, APIPaginatedResponse, ChannelType) @Eva Harris 
- feat(topics): add WAMP topic composition functions for chat subscriptions @Eva Harris 
- feat(types): add pagination and message list response types @Eva Harris 

#### Fixes

- fix(chat): match wire format — snake_case fields, integer type enums @Eva Harris 

#### Chores And Housekeeping

- chore(devenv): add devenv.nix environment @MakeShiftArtist 
- Chore(deps): Bump minimatch @dependabot[bot] 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump handlebars from 4.7.7 to 4.7.9 @dependabot[bot] 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump flatted from 3.3.3 to 3.4.2 @dependabot[bot] 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump picomatch from 2.3.1 to 2.3.2 @dependabot[bot] 
- Chore(deps-dev): Bump js-yaml from 4.1.0 to 4.1.1 @dependabot[bot] 

#### Refactoring and Updates

- refactor(types): remove dead APIPaginatedResponse type @Eva Harris 

#### General Changes

- use generic pagers @Eva Harris 
- fix chat proc ns @Eva Harris 
- get chat responce api @Eva Harris 
- json base type for kwargs @Eva Harris 
- fix chat paging @Eva Harris 
- ci(test): :construction_worker: Drop node 12 support @MakeShiftArtist
